The complete existing static suite and all 104 native T0/T1 catalogue groups pass on frozen-affine223-v1. The source-bound implementation remains the exact pinned research adapter; original supported selection, installation and arithmetic policy are unchanged. These checks cover syntax/instrument tests, planner controls, download/installer safety and native regressions. They do not qualify mixed-affine task quality, speed or a new product pack.

This supplement also preserves the duplicate-coalescing compatibility-failure output and the exact build preflight. The primary export/native/task source remains [[sources/runs/2026/10/2026-10-05-affine223-completed-screen]].

The frozen original low-budget/allocation pilot first stops at its five-minute quiet-start deadline without launching a model. One reset comes from Spotlight and the other four from the task app renderer, observed while large logs were displayed. It provides no timing outcome. The subsequent prospective mitigation retains every admission/exclusion condition, keeps displayed tool output small and permits one additional unchanged attempt after acceptance work. No system service or user app is killed or paused. A second failed admission would defer this speed comparison without changing allocation defaults.

Model-free native plans place the original ten-GB recipe at 821 expert slots, a 256-row prefill reserve and ordinary decoding; its fourteen-GB automatic/capped recipes leave 1635/1875 slots and 1024/512 prefill rows with streamed drafts. These are planned allocations, not throughput. A separate model-free ten-GB native-three-bit plan leaves 731 slots and a 256-row reserve while retaining streamed drafts. That difference motivates one prospectively frozen native-three-bit comparison only if the original ten-GB cell completes with eligible timing. Neither prepared experiment is completed evidence here. The existing pragmatic focused-quality limitations remain, and no one-pair result can promote a default.

Earlier original-pack experiments are reused rather than rerun: [[records/plan/whole-engine-optimization-2026-09-04]] records that load-verified expert packing lost all seven exact serving pairs and added startup cost despite positive isolated cold-read component results. [[records/decisions/draft-head-streams-its-experts-below-76-per-layer]] records that original streamed drafts and plain decoding tied at the previously tested ten-GB point. No new unchanged repack or forced-draft experiment follows from these known results.
